## 2026-11-04 主机远程桌面与截图方案（RD-01 调研交付）
- 需求：服务端随包远控服务、HostAgent随包被控端、Web控制端及独立截图，覆盖Windows/Linux/macOS；本轮仅评估，不实施/发布。交付docs/remote-desktop-feasibility.md，含六路线推荐分数、优劣/许可证、三平台边界、架构接入、截图和分期验收。
- 核对现有HostChannel/独立二进制回连协议/HostWorkspace及上游官方README/API/许可证。建议先原型MeshCentral/MeshAgent成套路线（Apache-2.0、Node及第二agent）；严格轻量则noVNC+受管VNC组件+Ferry中继（被控实现与许可证另核）。RustDesk OSS不等于官方自托管Web可免费内置。
- Windows用户会话/Session0及UAC、Linux X11/Wayland与无GUI、macOS TCC/FileVault边界已明确。截图可评估XCap或引擎当前帧；均未实机验证。Direct自审/需求核对完成；只改调研文档与私有MEMORY，未改业务代码/安装/提交推送/更新/发布。下一步为选型后的三平台原型，未作最终技术决策。

## GitHub Actions compatibility audit (2026-11-02, AUDIT phase; changes after audit, for alpha.33)
- User: wants no Ferry-imposed limits on act integration; agrees to remove the legacy JSON format; audit first, then change everything together; use Codex heavily.
- Clarified to user: the pipeline YAML is ONE standard GitHub Actions workflow (no custom syntax); Ferry stores host bindings/checkout bindings outside it, orchestrates jobs across hosts by `needs`, and sends the whole workflow + selected job to act per host.
- Known Ferry-imposed limits: actions.rs:114 (128KB), :123 (1-50 jobs), :279 (1-50 steps/job), static secrets/vars names only (snapshot only referenced secrets). Unknown: reusable workflows, services, concurrency, job-level continue-on-error, max-parallel, etc.
- C RESULTS (real server+Windows runner, data/maintenance/compat-e2e-result.json): OK = needs failure->skip + always()/failure() jobs, job-level if on github ctx, dynamic fromJSON matrix from needs, matrix leg outputs downstream, workflow env/defaults, literal secrets['X'] masked, max-parallel accepted. WRONG = job-level continue-on-error (dependant skipped, run failed; GitHub: tolerated), services silently ignored in host mode (job succeeded without service). FERRY-REJECTED = concurrency (workflow+job), environment, reusable workflows (uses: ./.github/workflows/x.yml), dynamic secrets[matrix.key], >50 jobs, >50 steps.
- AUDIT DONE: A (server) $TEMP/ferry-audit-a-result.md, B (worker, 56 PASS/10 FAIL/8 NS) $TEMP/ferry-audit-b-result.md. User chose: do BOTH batches, then release once (alpha.33), then update local 4291 + runner.
- IMPLEMENTATION PLAN (authoritative): data/maintenance/actions-compat-plan.md (contract, units W/S1/S2/L, order, status checklist).
- Units started: W = Codex in worktree $TEMP/ferry-w (branch feat/compat-w); S1 = Codex in $TEMP/ferry-s1 (branch feat/compat-s1). S2 = Claude after merge; L = Codex after S2.
- Units: A Codex read-only static audit of server (prompt $TEMP/ferry-audit-a-prompt.md, result $TEMP/ferry-audit-a-result.md); B Codex worker empirical tests in worktree $TEMP/ferry-audit-b (result $TEMP/ferry-audit-b-result.md); C me: real e2e on this Windows box (server+runner+worker) for orchestration features. Then: consolidated compatibility matrix -> user -> implement (remove legacy with migration, lift limits, dynamic secrets, fix gaps).

## Compose NATS auth bug (2026-11-01, shipped in alpha.29)
- User's real Compose run (alpha.27): ferry container restarts with "cannot connect to cluster NATS". ## ASPEED 监控断线 — 2026-10-30
- 根因：本机已安装 `alpha.13` 旧 Agent（Windows 服务 `runner-agent`）不识别新增的 `Monitor` 会话类型；主机监控每 30 秒请求一次，旧 Agent 解析未知控制帧后断开重连。`4291` 日志证实每分钟接入 30 秒后发生 WebSocket 无关闭握手重置。
- 修复：Agent 握手新增兼容的 `supports_monitor` 能力字段（旧 Agent 缺省 false），服务端对旧 Agent 拒绝详细监控会话并提示升级，避免发出未知帧。新 Agent 宣告支持；增加旧 Agent 不收帧的回归测试与协议兼容测试。
- 验证：协议 3 项、Runner 22 项、服务端定向回归、`cargo check --workspace --locked`、`cargo fmt --all -- --check`、`git diff --check` 通过。`4291` 已重建并启动为 PID 83708，ASPEED 连续在线超过两分钟，旧断线循环消失。旧 Agent 的详细图表仍须新版本安装后才可用。
- 另项：本机 `cargo test --workspace --locked` 因桌面升级器 Windows 命名管道权限测试 `exclusive_local_pipe_authenticates_both_peer_processes` 报 OS error 5 而失败；单独重跑仍失败。`cargo test -p ferry-server` 的 144 项中 141 通过、2 忽略、1 项 Actions checkout 脚本因本机缺少外部程序失败。两项未涉及本轮改动，发布前仍须复核 CI。自动执行审查拒绝了停止并重启 `5173` Vite 的诊断命令；未重试或绕过，当前 `5173` 保持运行。

## T16 implementation and verified stages
### Native service / task reservations
- Native systemd/launchd/WindowsSCM CLI install/start/stop/status/uninstall; foreground retained. Protected state ownership/ACL; duplicate install preserves executable/identity/runningservice; conflict paths rejected; uninstall preserves data.
- Windows PowerShell searchpath isolated; OWNER RIGHTS accepted only after owner trusted. Stop drains sessions, SCMStopPending checkpoints, native crash restart.
- runner_updates.rs fair perhost read/write reservations; wholeActions task holds leases across jobs. Task-local reuse prevents laterjob deadlock with queued upgrade writer; streams/PTYPumps retain leases. WaitingActions cancellation releases partialreservations without aborting activecleanup.
- CI40630691384 all3OSPASS. No whole-machine reboot acceptance.

### Signed updates / supervisor
- Release runner-update.json binds schema/protocol/version/five exact target artifacts/HTTPSURL/size/SHA256; signed with existing desktopMinisign key, independently verifies signature and exact asset catalog, package/Cargo/tag consistency.
- build.rs embeds fixed publickey and target; verification precedes metadata trust; rejects downgrade/wrongtarget/version/protocol/tampering/size/hash. Existing key supports fixture compile override only; runtime cannot replace it.
- Supervisor stable native entry launches immutable signed versions/<sha256>/runner. Worker stdoutJSON readiness follows authenticatedWelcome; parent persists journal then confirms via stdin; worker sends UpdateHealth only afterward.
- Parent holds stdin separately: Child.wait otherwise closes it. EOF graceful stop. WindowsJobObject killonclose prevents orphan worker; stderr protectedservice.log.
- Journal current/previous/pending/failed_version; atomic persisted state and durable staged directories (Unixfsync/WindowsMoveFileExWRITE_THROUGH). Pendingstartup rollback; candidatebadexec/wrongversion/60shealth timeout rollback. Corrupt/missing/nonexecutablecurrent falls backprevious/bootstrap.
- Native worker signedHTTPSdownload bounded300s/redirect5/nativeOSroots/signedsize, stagevalidate, drain, activation. No overwriting runningWindowsbinary.
- Control Update and reports UpdateHealth/UpdateFailed; Hello.managed_updates defaultfalse keeps oldclients/foreground compatible. GET/POST workspacehosts/id/runner-update permissions+tenantfilter; POSTcurrently explicitversion/manifest/signature, no releasediscovery/UI.
- Update job outlives HTTP disconnect; wholehostexclusive from drain through health/rollback.420stimeoutunknown retainslock.

### Server persistence / reviewed fixes
- Migration hosts.agent_update nullableJSONtext; GETdurable state. Initialadmission independenttask; persist beforedispatch. Startup recover before schedulers/listener acquires pendingexclusion; draining never-sent becomesfailed; updating/unknown awaits managedconfirmedhealth, version/failed_version reconciles.
- transition_update retriesDBwrite while caller retainslock; only durableterminal allows newtasks. Auditfailure cleanup uses same retry.
- Reviewer /root/review_runner_service found durability/currentfallback and serverpersistentstate issues (fixed), then P2audit+cleanupwrite failure leavingdraining (fixed18c2e68 after realSQLite RED0ea42c3). No newP1 in review; fullActions+upgrade acceptance required.

## Latest user steering — desktop upgrade / Windows UAC
User asks whether independent updater should stopoldservices then upgrade automatically without installerwizard/UACeverytime. Inspected desktop/tauri.conf.json: NSISinstallModeboth, updaterinstallModepassive; desktop/windows/installer.nsi /UPDATE enables passive+restart. This hides wizard but does not remove per-machineinstaller UAC.
Explained: dedicated upgrade executor is appropriate; graceful drain before stopping/replacing, health+rollback. Per-user upgrades normally no elevation; all-users needs pre-authorized protected updater service installed with initialadminapproval. Cannot bypass OS privilege enforcement; existing oldinstallation migration may require oneauthorization. AgentRunner already uses native service supervisor; desktop privilegedupdater is NOT implemented and must not be conflated. No new desktop code changed yet. Preserve originalT15/T16/release objective.

## Historical: Contribution analytics feasibility research (2026-11-02; superseded by implementation above)
- User requested research only: robotism/gitinsight, reuse repository connections/monitoring; prefer platform APIs without server clone, otherwise daily act pipeline, incremental/full options.
- Read upstream main at observed HEAD 25db35e909e10f6814bb0e8b28e360369cc8adbc: gitinsight.go clones/fetches branches; gitanalyze.go computes commit diffs; handle.go skips unchanged branches but reanalyzes/replaces configured history on changes. utils.go language metric is changed-file extension counts; effectives=max(additions-deletions,0), not code quality. CLI exposes serv/config, no existing one-shot analysis/export command.
- Ferry reuse: repository_auth.rs MonitorAccess handles connection auth/refresh/provider requests but commits() reads first page only (50); monitor/polling truncates displayed commits. Event history cannot serve as exhaustive contribution facts. cron_jobs.rs supports timezone-aware pipeline scheduling; actions_run.rs supports checkout and artifacts. No new analytics implementation made.
- Verified public GitHub single commit API returns stats + file additions/deletions; GitLab official commits docs support with_stats=true. GitHub official docs state commit files capped at 3000; aggregate stats may return 202 and some endpoints have repository-size limits. Codeup/Gitee/Gitea detailed stats completeness remains unverified.
- Recommendation (proposal, not approved implementation): API historical backfill + daily SHA/reachability-based incremental reconciliation, monitor events as dirty hints, workspace/repository/SHA idempotency and separate branch membership; act worker fallback for generic Git or explicitly unsupported/incomplete APIs, cached bare Git history and one-shot collector with structured result ingestion. Do not assume just yesterday timestamps are complete or daily full clone is necessary.
- Research complete. No builds/tests required for read-only research. Existing user edits retained. Next implementation requires agreed metrics, branch scope/history range, and provider priority; no implementation authorization inferred.
